# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 019 ORDINARY-COUNTRY MANAGEMENT
# ============================================================================
# Owns the baseline, Organized Muster, and Arsenal Lottery management surface.
# Every lot operation captures a stable lot UID, resolves the current row from
# that UID, and mutates the aligned ledgers only after the engine-side action
# succeeds. The paid-country request dispatcher hands composition to the
# unrestricted registry only at Evolution III; claimant logic and Evolution IV
# family logic live in their own files and are deliberately not implemented here.
# It also owns exact ordinary-country annex teardown, frozen proof sets, and the
# annexer-local retry queue used when engine deletion cannot be proven at once.
# ============================================================================

# `random_list` weights do not parse shared script-constant tokens.
@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = 1

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Telemetry, staged ideas, and shared accounting
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_successful_management_actions } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_successful_management_actions = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_failed_management_actions } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_failed_management_actions = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_integrated_lot_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_integrated_lot_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_standardized_lot_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardized_lot_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_demobilized_lot_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilized_lot_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_preserved_specialist_cadre_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_preserved_specialist_cadre_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_cannibalized_prototype_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_cannibalized_prototype_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_management_congestion_pressure } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_pressure = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_total_requests } } set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_requests = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_total_management_successes } } set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_management_successes = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_total_management_failures } } set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_management_failures = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_total_integrated_lots } } set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_integrated_lots = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_total_demobilized_lots } } set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_demobilized_lots = 0 } }
}

infantry_spawn_record_management_success = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_successful_management_actions = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_management_successes = 1 }
	}
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_succeeded
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_failed
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_partial
}

infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_failed_management_actions = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_management_failures = 1 }
	}
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_failed
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_succeeded
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_partial
}

infantry_spawn_record_integrated_lot = {
	infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_integrated_lot_count = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_integrated_lots = 1 }
	}
	infantry_spawn_achievement_record_current_lot_integration = yes
}

infantry_spawn_record_demobilized_lot = {
	infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilized_lot_count = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_demobilized_lots = 1 }
	}
}

# Called by the shared staged-pressure helper after its structural congestion
# pass and before tier ideas are selected. Persistent management relief and
# request pressure are applied here so recalculation remains idempotent.
infantry_spawn_apply_management_pressure_adjustments = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt < 0 } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability < 0 } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = 0 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief min = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.minimum max = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.persistent_relief_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_congestion_pressure min = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.minimum max = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.persistent_pressure_maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_congestion_pressure = infantry_spawn_request_count_generation }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_congestion_pressure = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.request_pressure }
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = infantry_spawn_request_congestion_pressure }
	# Every unresolved battalion-equivalent of manpower liability feeds the
	# existing congestion ladder until its exact obligation row is settled.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability_pressure = infantry_spawn_manpower_liability }
	divide_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_manpower_liability_pressure value = constant:infantry_spawn_accounting.manpower_liability_per_battalion if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = infantry_spawn_manpower_liability_pressure }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_supply_entries^infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.heavy compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.heavy_supply_lot_pressure }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_supply_entries^infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.locally_impossible compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.impossible_supply_lot_pressure }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index > 0 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.claimant_lot_pressure }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_pressure_lot_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_anomalous_family_congestion = infantry_spawn_active_anomalous_family_count }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_anomalous_family_congestion = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.anomalous_family_pressure }
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = infantry_spawn_anomalous_family_congestion }
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = infantry_spawn_management_congestion_pressure }
	subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_scenario_pressure_floor
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_army_congestion value = infantry_spawn_scenario_pressure_floor compare = less_than }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_army_congestion = infantry_spawn_scenario_pressure_floor }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_army_congestion min = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.minimum max = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_muster_control min = constant:infantry_spawn_control.minimum max = constant:infantry_spawn_control.maximum }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stable selected-lot lookup and AI target choice
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index_is_valid = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid = 1 }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_select_first_auditing_ordinary_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.auditing }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_select_next_unresolved_ordinary_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_lot_count = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index_is_valid = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_rows_checked = 0 }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_rows_checked value = infantry_spawn_management_lot_count compare = less_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_management_lot_count compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_rows_checked = 1 }
	}
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
}

infantry_spawn_select_weakest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = constant:infantry_spawn_control.maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_current_score value = infantry_spawn_management_best_score compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = infantry_spawn_management_current_score }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_current_score value = infantry_spawn_management_best_score compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = infantry_spawn_management_current_score }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
}

# AI parity for standardization: only an exact, affordable conversion candidate
# can win the score. The final refresh leaves the same cost snapshot used by
# the player decision and the start effect.
infantry_spawn_select_strongest_standardizable_ordinary_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index < constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index < 1 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index < 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
			if = {
				limit = { infantry_spawn_can_execute_selected_lot_standardization = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_current_score value = infantry_spawn_management_best_score compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = infantry_spawn_management_current_score }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_best_lot_index }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
}

infantry_spawn_select_specialist_unresolved_ordinary_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_management_best_score compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unmanaged_prototype_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.fully_random compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.unmanaged }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index > 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_current_score value = infantry_spawn_management_best_score compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = infantry_spawn_management_current_score }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
}

infantry_spawn_select_most_unsustainable_prototype_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.fully_random compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.unmanaged }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.preserved }
				}
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index > 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_manpower_score = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			divide_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_prototype_manpower_score value = constant:infantry_spawn_accounting.manpower_liability_per_battalion if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_prototype_manpower_score }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_supply_score = infantry_spawn_lot_supply_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_supply_score = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.unsustainable_supply_score }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_prototype_supply_score }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_mismatch_score = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_mismatch_score = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.unsustainable_mismatch_factor }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = infantry_spawn_prototype_mismatch_score }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.preserved } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_current_score = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.unsustainable_preserved_bonus }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_current_score value = infantry_spawn_management_best_score compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_best_score = infantry_spawn_management_current_score }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixed management payments derived from the shared request-cost table
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_pay_base_army_experience_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_army_experience }
	army_experience = infantry_spawn_negative_payment
}

infantry_spawn_pay_base_infantry_equipment_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_infantry_equipment }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_negative_payment }
}

infantry_spawn_pay_base_support_equipment_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_support_equipment }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_negative_payment }
}

infantry_spawn_pay_base_trains_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_trains }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_negative_payment }
}

infantry_spawn_pay_base_fuel_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_fuel }
	add_fuel = infantry_spawn_negative_payment
}

infantry_spawn_pay_prototype_preservation_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.preserve_army_experience_cost }
	army_experience = infantry_spawn_negative_payment
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.preserve_support_equipment_cost }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_negative_payment }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.preserve_train_cost }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_negative_payment }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_negative_payment = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.preserve_fuel_cost }
	add_fuel = infantry_spawn_negative_payment
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Audit, role assignment, training, and integration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_start_selected_lot_audit = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_first_auditing_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.auditing }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
		}
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_army_experience_cost = yes
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_support_equipment_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_base_days }
		if = {
			limit = { is_major = yes }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_large_country_bonus_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_war = yes }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_war_penalty_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_stacked }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_stacked_penalty_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_has_evolution_i = yes }
			multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
			round_variable = infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days < constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_minimum_days } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_minimum_days }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_formation_roll_call_mission
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
}

infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_audit = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.auditing }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.open }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.audited }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { infantry_spawn_control_is_low = yes }
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_appointed
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.audit_success }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.audit_relief }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_organized_success_package
						infantry_spawn_has_evolution_i = yes
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.territorial_assignment }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.audit_relief }
			}
			infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.territorial_assignment }
			infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_partial
		}
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.failed_mission_penalty }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_pressure = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.audit_relief }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_organized_success_package
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_assign_selected_lot_territorial_role = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_weakest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
		}
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_support_equipment_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.territorial }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.territorial_assignment }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.territorial_relief }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
		infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
		infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_start_selected_lot_training = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_training_cycle_running }
		}
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_infantry_equipment_cost = yes
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_support_equipment_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_training_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_training_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.training_cycle_days }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_training_cycle_running
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_training_ground_saturation
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_training_cycle_mission
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
}

infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_training = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_training_target_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
			OR = {
				NOT = { infantry_spawn_control_is_low = yes }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_appointed
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.field_ready compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.territorial_assignment }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.failed_mission_penalty }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_training_ground_saturation
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_training_cycle_running
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_training_target_lot_uid
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_start_selected_lot_standardization = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_strongest_standardizable_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_start_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			infantry_spawn_can_execute_selected_lot_standardization = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
		}
		infantry_spawn_ensure_canonical_standard_template = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_preflight_standardization_conversion = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready > 0 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.standardization_days }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_accelerated_standardization_available
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.coherent compare = less_than } }
				}
				multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
				round_variable = infantry_spawn_standardization_mission_days
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_appointed }
				multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
				round_variable = infantry_spawn_standardization_mission_days
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_standardization_running
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_training_ground_saturation
			activate_mission = infantry_spawn_standardization_cycle_mission
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_start_succeeded = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_start_succeeded = 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids
}

infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_standardization = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_completed = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_debited = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
			OR = {
				NOT = { infantry_spawn_control_is_low = yes }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_appointed
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_ensure_canonical_standard_template = yes
		infantry_spawn_preflight_standardization_conversion = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready > 0 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_row_index }
			infantry_spawn_build_standardization_loss_for_current_lot = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_ready
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_invalid_profile }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_target_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_manpower < 1 }
					infantry_spawn_exact_profile_totals_are_affordable = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment = 1 }
				infantry_spawn_apply_exact_profile_totals_to_country = yes
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_debited = 1 }
				infantry_spawn_convert_preflighted_lot_to_canonical_template = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_postproof_failed = 0 } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_achievement_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid }
					infantry_spawn_achievement_disqualify_units_in_current_lot = yes
					infantry_spawn_commit_preflighted_standardization_conversion = yes
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.standardized }
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.standardization_success }
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.standardization_relief }
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardized_lot_count = 1 }
					infantry_spawn_record_integrated_lot = yes
					infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
					infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_completed = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					infantry_spawn_rollback_preflighted_standardization_conversion = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_rollback_succeeded = 0 } }
						infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_completed = 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_debited > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment = 0 }
			infantry_spawn_apply_exact_profile_totals_to_country = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.failed_mission_penalty }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_training_ground_saturation
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_standardization_running
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_common_tables_cycle
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_emergency_integrate_selected_lot = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.integrated }
		infantry_spawn_achievement_mark_emergency_integration = yes
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.emergency_integration_penalty }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_emergency_integration_burden_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.training_cycle_days }
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = infantry_spawn_emergency_integration_burden_active
			days = infantry_spawn_emergency_integration_burden_days
			value = 1
		}
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_officer_reassignment_burden
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_training_ground_saturation
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_supply_allocation_strain
		infantry_spawn_record_integrated_lot = yes
		infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
		infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

# Inputs: infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile and
# infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount. Every supported profile maps to the
# exact HOI4 archetype used by the obligation row; there is no proxy branch.

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_manpower = infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.infantry_equipment compare = equals } }

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.artillery_equipment compare = equals } }

# Input: infantry_spawn_lot_row_index. Rebuilds the conversion-loss snapshot
# from settled, unit-backed salvageable-paid rows for that exact lot.
infantry_spawn_build_standardization_loss_for_current_lot = {
	infantry_spawn_clear_exact_profile_totals = yes
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_lot_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_unit_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_obligation_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_template_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_set_invalid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index > 0 }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_generation_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_template_uid compare = equals }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index } }
					}
					add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_set_invalid = 1 } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index > 0 }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.settled }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index > 0 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
				}
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_row_count = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile = infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index }
				infantry_spawn_add_current_profile_to_exact_totals = yes
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_invalid_profile }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_set_invalid = 0 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids^num > 0 }
			}
			infantry_spawn_scale_exact_material_totals_for_standardization = yes
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_ready
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_loss_unit_uids
}


# Rebuilds a country-persistent cost snapshot for the currently selected lot.
# Raw quantities remain profile-specific while debt-value and manpower sums
# are checked against their lot accounting rows.
infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = {
	infantry_spawn_clear_exact_profile_totals = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index_is_valid = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilized compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.unaccounted }
					}
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_count = 1 }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_scan_index = 1 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index > 0 }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.outstanding }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.servicing }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_row_count = 1 }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_debt_value = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile = infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
				infantry_spawn_add_current_profile_to_exact_totals = yes
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_row_count > 0 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_invalid_profile }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_debt_value value = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower value = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_equipment_debt value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_debt_value compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_manpower_liability value = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted } }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_count > 0 }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_division_count value = infantry_spawn_exact_unaccounted_unit_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count > 0 }
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_ready
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_row_count > 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index > 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index > 0 }
				}
			}
			infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < 1 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
		infantry_spawn_build_standardization_loss_for_current_lot = yes
	}
}

# Applies the rebuilt exact profile totals. Payment subtracts exact archetype
# stock, fuel, and manpower; salvage adds the same archetypes back.
# Input: infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment.
infantry_spawn_apply_exact_profile_totals_to_country = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_manpower > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower } }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_infantry_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_infantry_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_infantry_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_artillery_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_artillery_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_artillery_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_support_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_support_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_support_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_motorized_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_motorized_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_motorized_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_armored_car_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_armored_car_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_armored_car_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = armored_car_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_mechanized_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_mechanized_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_mechanized_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = mechanized_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_helicopter_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_helicopter_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_helicopter_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = helicopter_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_amphibious_mechanized_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_amphibious_mechanized_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_amphibious_mechanized_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = amphibious_mechanized_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_fuel > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_fuel }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_fuel } }
		add_fuel = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_train_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_train_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_train_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_coal_golem_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_coal_golem_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_coal_golem_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_light_tank_chassis > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_light_tank_chassis }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_light_tank_chassis } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = light_tank_chassis amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_light_tank_artillery_chassis > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_light_tank_artillery_chassis }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_light_tank_artillery_chassis } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = light_tank_artillery_chassis amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_anti_tank_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_anti_tank_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_anti_tank_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_tank_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_anti_air_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_anti_air_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_anti_air_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_rocket_artillery_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_rocket_artillery_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_rocket_artillery_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = rocket_artillery_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_amphibious_tank_chassis > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_amphibious_tank_chassis }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_amphibious_tank_chassis } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = amphibious_tank_chassis amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_armored_support_vehicle > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_armored_support_vehicle }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_armored_support_vehicle } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = armored_support_vehicle amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {

			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_gas_mask_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_gas_mask_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_gas_mask_equipment } }
