		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = gas_mask_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_decontamination_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_decontamination_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_decontamination_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = decontamination_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_cbrn_instrument_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_cbrn_instrument_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_cbrn_instrument_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = cbrn_instrument_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_clone_equipment > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_clone_equipment }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_clone_equipment } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = clone_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_robot_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_robot_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_robot_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = kruger_robot_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_teleportation_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_teleportation_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_teleportation_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = teleportation_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_temporal_equipment_1 > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = 0 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_temporal_equipment_1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_kruger_temporal_equipment_1 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = kruger_temporal_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount = infantry_spawn_exact_super_heavy_tank_destroyer_chassis } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = super_heavy_tank_destroyer_chassis amount = infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_amount }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Canonical National Muster Standard and proof-backed cohort conversion
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Validates the one shared country template against both engine state and the
# aligned Event 19 template/component ledgers. The canonical row deliberately
# owns lot uid 0, so no per-lot teardown can ever treat it as a unique source.
# Inputs: infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid and
# infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid. A generated template UID is
# transaction-safe only when exactly one lot row and one template row own it,
# and every unit-ledger reference belongs to that same lot.
infantry_spawn_prove_source_template_unique_to_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_template_is_unique = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_rows = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_rows = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_invalid = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_lot_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_template_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_unit_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_rows = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid compare = equals } } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_invalid = 1 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_rows = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid compare = equals } } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_invalid = 1 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid compare = equals }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid compare = equals } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_invalid = 1 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_invalid = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_rows = 1 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_rows = 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_template_is_unique = 1 }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_validate_canonical_standard_template = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_canonical_component_positions
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_template_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_template_component_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid > 0 }
			has_template = "National Muster Standard"
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.integrated }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_row_index = infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_count = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_primary_count = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_secondary_count = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_invalid = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid compare = equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_count = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_position = infantry_spawn_template_component_column_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index }
					multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_position = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_primary_column }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_position = infantry_spawn_template_component_row_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index }
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_slot_kind_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_component_kind.combat }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_unit_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_component_token.infantry }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_row_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
							NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_canonical_component_positions value = infantry_spawn_canonical_component_position } }
							OR = {
								AND = {
									check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_column_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_manifest_column.first }
									check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_row_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_primary_column compare = less_than }
								}
								AND = {
									check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_column_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_manifest_column.second }
									check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_row_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_secondary_column compare = less_than }
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_positions = infantry_spawn_canonical_component_position }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_column_entries^infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_manifest_column.first } }
							add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_primary_count = 1 }
						}
						else = { add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_secondary_count = 1 } }
					}
					else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_invalid = 1 } }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_scan_index = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_invalid = 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_combat }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_primary_count = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_primary_column }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_secondary_count = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_secondary_column }
				}
				set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "National Muster Standard" force_allow_recruiting = no }
				set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "National Muster Standard" is_locked = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_canonical_component_positions
}

# Creates the canonical template only when its stable name is absent and all
# destination arrays are aligned. An existing name without our uid is a hard
# invariant failure; no fallback template is created.
infantry_spawn_ensure_canonical_standard_template = {
	infantry_spawn_validate_canonical_standard_template = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready = 0 }
			NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
			NOT = { has_template = "National Muster Standard" }
			infantry_spawn_template_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_template_component_ledger_is_aligned = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
		}
		infantry_spawn_allocate_template_uid = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
		division_template = {
			name = "National Muster Standard"
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = no
			priority = 0
			regiments = {
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 3 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 4 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_status = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.integrated }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_recruitment_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
		infantry_spawn_append_current_template_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_manifest_component_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_component_token.infantry }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_manifest_component_column = constant:infantry_spawn_manifest_column.first }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_manifest_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_primary_column }
		infantry_spawn_append_current_combat_column_manifest = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_manifest_component_column = constant:infantry_spawn_manifest_column.second }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_manifest_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_family_size.line_infantry_secondary_column }
		infantry_spawn_append_current_combat_column_manifest = yes
		infantry_spawn_validate_canonical_standard_template = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready = 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

# Input: infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid. Proves the
# exact lot, generation, source-template uid, unit-ledger uid set, and live
# division uid set before any resource or division mutation.
infantry_spawn_preflight_standardization_conversion = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_ready > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_canonical_template_uid = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_target_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_prove_source_template_unique_to_lot = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_template_is_unique > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid > 0 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.retired compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_row_index = infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index > 0 }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_generation_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid compare = equals }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index } }
					}
					add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 1 } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_unit_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = 1 }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_generation_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
					NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid } }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids^num < 1 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 1 }
		}
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_failed = 1 } }
}

# Converts only the exact preflighted live uid set. The post-proof records count
# and identity independently before any persistent lot/unit ledger rewrite.
infantry_spawn_convert_preflighted_lot_to_canonical_template = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_divisions_before = num_divisions }
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
			is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
		}
		change_division_template = { division_template = "National Muster Standard" }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_canonical_template_uid }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_postproof_failed = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = 1 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_generation_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_canonical_template_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
				is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid } }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_postproof_failed = 1 } }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid }
	infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_standardization_divisions_before compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { has_template = "National Muster Standard" }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists = 0 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_postproof_failed = 1 }
	}
}

# Restores the exact cohort to its old dynamic template if conversion proof
# fails. Resource refund is orchestrated by the caller after this proof.
infantry_spawn_rollback_preflighted_standardization_conversion = {
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			every_country_division = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_canonical_template_uid compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
				}
				change_division_template = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [SOURCE_TEMPLATE_UID]" }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid }
			}
		}
		SOURCE_TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_rollback_succeeded = 1 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = 1 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_generation_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
				is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid } }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_rollback_succeeded = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_live_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_standardization_expected_unit_uids^num compare = equals } } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_rollback_succeeded = 0 }
	}
}

# Success-only ledger publication. The shared canonical row remains lot uid 0;
# only this lot and its active unit rows are repointed to the canonical uid.
infantry_spawn_commit_preflighted_standardization_conversion = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_row_index }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_commit_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_standardization_commit_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_commit_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_standardization_preflight_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_commit_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_commit_unit_index = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_commit_unit_index = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.retired }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [SOURCE_TEMPLATE_UID]" force_allow_recruiting = no }
			set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [SOURCE_TEMPLATE_UID]" is_locked = yes }
		}
		SOURCE_TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_standardization_source_template_uid|.0]"
	}
}


infantry_spawn_select_next_unaccounted_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_count = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index_is_valid = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_rows_checked = 0 }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_rows_checked value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_count compare = less_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_count compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_scan_index = 1 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_rows_checked = 1 }
	}
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
}

# AI uses the same affordability trigger and exact transaction as the player.
infantry_spawn_select_first_affordable_exact_obligation_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_previous_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_lot_found = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_pass = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_lot_found = 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_pass < 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_lot_found = 0 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_pass = 0 }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_pass = 1 }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilized compare = less_than }
						}
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index > 0 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index > 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index }
				infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
				if = {
					limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_exact_obligations_are_affordable = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_lot_found = 1 }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_scan_pass = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_lot_found = 0 } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index = infantry_spawn_exact_previous_selected_lot_index }
		infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
	}
}

# Input: infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index. The proof is entirely
# read-only; the commit below has no conditional failure after mutation begins.
infantry_spawn_preflight_paid_unaccounted_lot_finalization = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_valid = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_uids
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index < 1 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index < 1 }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_allow_pending_payment > 0 }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_debt_value compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower compare = equals }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_count = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_invalid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.unaccounted }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index > 0 }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_uid compare = equals }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index } }
					}
					add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_count = 1 }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_invalid = 1 } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_invalid = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_count > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_count compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_division_count value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > 0 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid compare = equals } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_row_index = infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_valid = 1 }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_commit_preflighted_unaccounted_lot_finalization = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_uid compare = equals }
				is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.unaccounted }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.unaccounted_settled }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_index = 1 }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_division_count = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_count }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted_settled }
	subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count = 1 }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.retired }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" force_allow_recruiting = no }
			set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" is_locked = yes }
		}
		TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_template_uid|.0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_division_count < 1 } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_division_count = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_formation_detected
	}
}

infantry_spawn_finalize_paid_unaccounted_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_allow_pending_payment = 0 }
	infantry_spawn_preflight_paid_unaccounted_lot_finalization = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_valid > 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_commit_preflighted_unaccounted_lot_finalization = yes
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_uids
}

# Proves the complete full-payment row set and any terminal unaccounted
# disposition before country resources are debited. The following commit scans
# this frozen uid set and contains no branch that can fail after debit.
infantry_spawn_preflight_exact_settlement_commit = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_succeeded = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_valid = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_row_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_terminal_row_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_debt_value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_manpower = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_obligation_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_all_obligation_uids
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_uid compare = equals } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index > 0 }
					NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_all_obligation_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index } }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_all_obligation_uids = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index > 0 }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.outstanding }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.servicing }
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_obligation_uids = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_row_count = 1 }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_debt_value = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_manpower = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 0 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 0 }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.settled }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.forfeited }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.transferred }
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_terminal_row_count = 1 }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 1 } }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 1 } }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed > 0 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_row_count value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_row_count compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_debt_value value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_debt_value compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_manpower value = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_target_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_uid compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_allow_pending_payment = 1 }
		infantry_spawn_preflight_paid_unaccounted_lot_finalization = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_allow_pending_payment = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_valid = 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_failed = 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_succeeded = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_settle_selected_lot_exact_obligations_unlocked = {
	infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_exact_obligations_are_affordable = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_index = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_index }
		infantry_spawn_preflight_exact_settlement_commit = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_preflight_succeeded > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment = 1 }
			infantry_spawn_apply_exact_profile_totals_to_country = yes
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_debt_value }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = infantry_spawn_exact_manpower }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_index = 0 }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_index = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_lot_uid compare = equals }
						is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_obligation_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_amount = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index }
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_amount }
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_amount }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 0 }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 0 }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.settled }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_obligation_scan_index = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_valid > 0 } }
				infantry_spawn_commit_preflighted_unaccounted_lot_finalization = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_succeeded = 1 }
			infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
			infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_succeeded = 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_commit_obligation_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_all_obligation_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_unit_uids
	infantry_spawn_select_next_unaccounted_lot = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_index_is_valid = yes } }
		infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
		infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
	}
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

# Public settlement entry point. Exact obligations cannot debit the country or
# publish ledger changes while a same-tag scenario transaction owns the view.
infantry_spawn_settle_selected_lot_exact_obligations = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_settle_selected_lot_exact_obligations_unlocked = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact-cohort supervised demobilization and specialist preservation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_initialize_teardown_salvage = {
	infantry_spawn_clear_exact_profile_totals = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_manpower = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_infantry_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_artillery_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_support_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_motorized_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_armored_car_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_mechanized_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_helicopter_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_amphibious_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_tank_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_fuel = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_trains = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_light_tank_chassis = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_light_sp_chassis = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_anti_tank_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_anti_air_equipment = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_salvage_rocket_artillery_equipment = 0 }
}

# Derives recoverable resources from paid, unit-backed rows only. Ordinary
# demobilization applies the demobilization ratio to every exact profile;
# specialist preservation applies its ratio only to support-equipment rows.
# Lot-level incident obligations never become physical salvage.
infantry_spawn_build_exact_teardown_salvage = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_paid_manpower = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index > 0 }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.settled }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index > 0 }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_resource_profile.africa_pan_sappers_equipment_1 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_resource_profile.africa_oracle_recon_equipment_1 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_resource_profile.africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only = 0 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.support_equipment }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile = infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only > 0 }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_paid_manpower = infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index }
					multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_share }
					round_temp_variable = infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount > 0 } }
						infantry_spawn_add_current_profile_to_exact_totals = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_paid_manpower > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_total_component_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_total_component_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_total_component_count > 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_specialist_paid_manpower }
				multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				divide_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount value = infantry_spawn_specialist_total_component_count if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
				multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount = infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_share }
				round_temp_variable = infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_amount > 0 } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_current_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower }
					infantry_spawn_add_current_profile_to_exact_totals = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Technical cleanup and the explicit prototype-cannibalization disposition are
# the only teardown paths allowed to forfeit obligations. Ordinary player
# demobilization and specialist preservation remain preflight-gated on a zero
# exact balance. Lot-level rows (unit uid 0) are included in this single pass.
infantry_spawn_forfeit_remaining_lot_obligations_for_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition = constant:infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition.prototype_cannibalization }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index > 0 }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.outstanding }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.servicing }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_outstanding = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_debt_value = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
					subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = infantry_spawn_teardown_outstanding }
					subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_teardown_outstanding }
				}
				else = {
					subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = infantry_spawn_teardown_debt_value }
					subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_teardown_debt_value }
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = 0 }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = 0 }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.forfeited }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_scan_index = 1 }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_grant_controlled_teardown_salvage = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_transfer_is_payment = 0 }
	infantry_spawn_apply_exact_profile_totals_to_country = yes
}

# Inputs: infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid,
# infantry_spawn_teardown_include_claimant_loyal, and
# infantry_spawn_teardown_allow_absent_units. The preflight proves one unique
# locked source template, the exact generation/template/unit uid set, and the
# matching live cohort before the single template batch deletion. The shared
# canonical template is explicitly forbidden. Absent units are accepted only
# by annex cleanup, where the engine already removed the defeated army.
infantry_spawn_preflight_exact_lot_teardown = {
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_uids
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_lock_overlap = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 }
			has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_lock_overlap = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition = constant:infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition.prototype_cannibalization } }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_uniqueness_template_uid = infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid }
	infantry_spawn_prove_source_template_unique_to_lot = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid }
	infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_source_template_is_unique > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.retired compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.spawn_only }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_template_row_index = infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 } }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active compare = equals }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_include_claimant_loyal > 0 }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.claimant_loyal compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index > 0 }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_generation_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid compare = equals }
					NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index } }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count = 1 }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 } }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_unit_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index > 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.settled }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index > 0 }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index } }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_preflight_obligation_index = 1 }
	}
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = 1 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_teardown_generation_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid compare = equals }
				is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid } }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active compare = equals }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_include_claimant_loyal > 0 }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.claimant_loyal compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count = 1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count < 1 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count compare = equals } }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count > 0 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count value = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count compare = equals } }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count < 1 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_allow_absent_units = 0 }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count > 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists = 0 }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
	}
}

# A proven unique source template allows one documented delete_units operation.
# No unit-ledger row, lot count, template status, or salvage is committed until
# the exact division delta and complete target-lot identity absence both pass.
infantry_spawn_execute_exact_lot_teardown = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	infantry_spawn_initialize_teardown_salvage = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_batch_proven = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_deleted_unit_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		infantry_spawn_preflight_exact_lot_teardown = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 } }
			infantry_spawn_build_exact_teardown_salvage = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_exact_settlement_invalid_profile }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_count > 0 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup > 0 }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_divisions_before = num_divisions }
					meta_effect = {
						text = { delete_units = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" disband = no } }
						TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid|.0]"
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_delete_delta = infantry_spawn_teardown_divisions_before }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_delete_delta = num_divisions }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_remaining_identity_count = 0 }
