					every_country_division = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_remaining_identity_count = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_teardown_delete_delta value = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count compare = equals }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_remaining_identity_count = 0 }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_batch_proven = 1 }
					}
					else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 } }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_lock_overlap = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_allow_absent_units > 0 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_batch_proven = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_batch_proven > 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
						is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup > 0 } }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.destroyed }
					}
					else = { set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.demobilized } }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_scan_index = 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_deleted_unit_count = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_count }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_teardown_lot_row_index }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count = infantry_spawn_teardown_deleted_unit_count }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 } }
			infantry_spawn_forfeit_remaining_lot_obligations_for_cleanup = yes
		}
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 } }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_teardown_lot_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup > 0 } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.destroyed }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilized } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count = 1 } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_template_status.retired }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_teardown_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_recruitment_mode.locked_pending }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > 0 } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" force_allow_recruiting = no }
					set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" is_locked = yes }
				}
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_teardown_template_uid|.0]"
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 } }
			infantry_spawn_grant_controlled_teardown_salvage = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition = constant:infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition.prototype_cannibalization } }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.cannibalized }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_cannibalized_prototype_count = 1 }
				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.cannibalization_control_loss }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.cannibalization_congestion_relief }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.serviceable compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.substantially_equipped compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.cannibalization_research_bonus }
					infantry_spawn_grant_prototype_research_insight = yes
				}
				infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.controlled_demobilization }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.demobilization_relief }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only > 0 } }
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_preserved_specialist_cadre_count = 1 }
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_specialist_cadre_preserved
					add_timed_idea = { idea = infantry_spawn_preserved_specialist_training days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.training_cycle_days }
				}
				infantry_spawn_record_demobilized_lot = yes
				infantry_spawn_achievement_record_supervised_demobilized_lot = yes
			}
		}
		infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 } }
			infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_lock_overlap = 0 } }
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
			infantry_spawn_achievement_mark_teardown_invariant_failure = yes
		}
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_teardown_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_teardown_live_unit_uids
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count < 0 } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count = 0 } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
	}
	infantry_spawn_refresh_preserved_prototype_idea = yes
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_start_supervised_demobilization = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_start_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_use_preselected_lot > 0 } }
		}
		infantry_spawn_select_weakest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
	}
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_allow_selected_anomalous_family > 0 }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_selected_family_id compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 0 }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_demobilization_running }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.demobilization_days }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_resistance_days = infantry_spawn_lot_demobilization_resistance_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_resistance_days = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.demobilization_resistance_days }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_mission_days = infantry_spawn_demobilization_resistance_days }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_demobilization_pending
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_demobilization_running
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_supervised_demobilization_mission
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_start_succeeded = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_caller_handles_failure > 0 } } }
			infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_complete_supervised_demobilization = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_share = constant:infantry_spawn_accounting.demobilization_salvage_share }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_include_claimant_loyal = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition = constant:infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition.ordinary }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_allow_absent_units = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = division_template_lock_event_active } }
		infantry_spawn_execute_exact_lot_teardown = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_lock_overlap = 1 }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_demobilization_pending
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_demobilization_running
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_start_specialist_preservation = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_specialist_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index < 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_specialist_preservation_running }
		}
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_support_equipment_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.standardization_days }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_resistance_days = infantry_spawn_lot_demobilization_resistance_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_resistance_days = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.demobilization_resistance_days }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_specialist_mission_days = infantry_spawn_demobilization_resistance_days }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_demobilization_pending
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_specialist_preservation_running
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_specialist_preservation_mission
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
}

infantry_spawn_complete_specialist_preservation = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_specialist_target_lot_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_share = constant:infantry_spawn_accounting.cannibalization_salvage_share }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_include_claimant_loyal = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition = constant:infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition.ordinary }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_allow_absent_units = 0 }
	infantry_spawn_execute_exact_lot_teardown = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_failed > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_specialist_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_demobilization_pending
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_specialist_preservation_running
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_specialist_target_lot_uid
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Generation closeout
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = {
	# An unverified disappearance remains unresolved until every exact lot
	# obligation has been paid. Once the ledger reaches zero, archive the lot
	# without reporting a destruction or a supervised demobilization.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index < 1 }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index < 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_finalize_lot_index = infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index }
			infantry_spawn_finalize_paid_unaccounted_lot = yes
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unaccounted_lot_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.open }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.audited }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_closeout_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_has_unresolved_lot = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_has_unresolved_lot = 0 }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_closeout_generation_uid compare = equals }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.auditing }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.isolated }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.claimant_controlled }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.anomalous_containment }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.unaccounted }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_has_unresolved_lot = 1 }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_scan_index = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_has_unresolved_lot = 0 } }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.resolved }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count = 1 } }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_last_closed_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_closeout_generation_uid }
				infantry_spawn_achievement_evaluate_closed_generation = yes
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_closeout_ready
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_open_generation_exists = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_open_generation_exists = 0 }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.open }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.audited }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_open_generation_exists = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_open_generation_exists = 0 } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_audit_active
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_stacked
	}
}

infantry_spawn_start_muster_districts = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_muster_districts_running } }
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_army_experience_cost = yes
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_support_equipment_cost = yes
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_trains_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.officer_search_days }
		if = { limit = { is_major = yes } add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_large_country_bonus_days } }
		if = { limit = { infantry_spawn_congestion_is_severe = yes } add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_stacked_penalty_days } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_appointed }
			multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
			round_variable = infantry_spawn_muster_district_mission_days
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_muster_districts_running
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_officer_reassignment_burden
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_muster_districts_mission
	}
}

infantry_spawn_complete_muster_districts = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_muster_district_assigned_lot_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_assigned_lot_count = 0 } }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_assigned_this_cycle = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.claimant_controlled compare = less_than }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.local_authority }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_assigned_lot_count = 1 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_assigned_this_cycle = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_assigned_this_cycle > 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_muster_district_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_count = 0 } }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_district_count = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.audit_success }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.rail_corridor_relief }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_muster_districts_established
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_future_claimant_district_asset
		infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.failed_mission_penalty }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_officer_reassignment_burden
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_muster_districts_running
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_start_integration_staff_search = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_search_running } }
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_army_experience_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_officer_search_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.officer_search_days }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_search_running
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_officer_reassignment_burden
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_officer_search_mission
	}
}

infantry_spawn_complete_integration_staff_search = {
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_appointed
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_capacity } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_integration_staff_capacity = 0 } }
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_integration_staff_capacity = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.territorial_assignment }
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_officer_reassignment_burden
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_integration_staff_search_running
	infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_issue_common_tables = {
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_common_tables_cycle
	infantry_spawn_start_selected_lot_standardization = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_standardization_running } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_common_tables_cycle
	}
}

infantry_spawn_recognize_selected_emergency_reserve = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training compare = less_than }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.integrated }
		infantry_spawn_achievement_mark_emergency_integration = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_emergency_reserve_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.field_ready compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 1 }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_emergency_reserve_recognized
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_future_claimant_reserve_asset
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.territorial_assignment }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.territorial_relief }
		infantry_spawn_record_integrated_lot = yes
		infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
		infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Evolution II organization actions

infantry_spawn_register_reserved_rail_corridor_state = {
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_reserved_rail_corridor_state
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_owner = ROOT }
	ROOT = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_state_entries
						value = event_target:infantry_spawn_current_reserved_rail_corridor_state
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_state_entries
				value = event_target:infantry_spawn_current_reserved_rail_corridor_state
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_start_rail_corridor_mission = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_running }
		}
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_trains_cost = yes
		infantry_spawn_pay_base_fuel_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_target_state = infantry_spawn_lot_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		infantry_spawn_achievement_start_rail_proof = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.rail_corridor_days }
		if = { limit = { is_major = yes } add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_large_country_bonus_days } }
		if = { limit = { infantry_spawn_congestion_is_severe = yes } add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.audit_stacked_penalty_days } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_running
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_supply_allocation_strain
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_mission
	}
}

infantry_spawn_complete_rail_corridor_mission = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_target_state }
		var:infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_target_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor
				infantry_spawn_register_reserved_rail_corridor_state = yes
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_succeeded = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_succeeded > 0 } }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_relief = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.rail_corridor_relief }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_count } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_count = 0 } }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_count = 1 }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_secured
		infantry_spawn_achievement_complete_rail_proof = yes
		infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.failed_mission_penalty }
		infantry_spawn_achievement_mark_rail_proof_failure = yes
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_supply_allocation_strain
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_running
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_target_state
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

# Public active-entry hooks. Root evolution dispatch may call these repeatedly;
# they are deliberately idempotent and never create a second mission.
infantry_spawn_evolution_one_reevaluate_management = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_has_evolution_i = yes }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_evolution_one_management_active
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running } set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_organized_success_package }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_accelerated_standardization_available
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_coherent_lot_exists = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_coherent_lot_exists = 0 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.integrated compare = less_than }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.coherent compare = less_than } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_coherent_lot_exists = 1 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_coherent_lot_exists > 0 } }
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_accelerated_standardization_available
		}
	}
}

# Applies the visible reinforcement burden only while an exact, live preserved
# prototype remains in the ordinary-country ledger.
infantry_spawn_abort_prototype_maintenance_trial = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_running
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid
			infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.maintenance_trial }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.unmanaged }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than } }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed } }
		}
	}
	remove_mission = infantry_spawn_prototype_maintenance_trial_mission
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_running
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_prototype_completion_pending
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_management_available
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_preservation_available
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_cannibalization_available
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_prototype_lot_uid
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_prototype_live_unit_uids
}

infantry_spawn_refresh_preserved_prototype_idea = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_live_preserved_prototype_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.preserved }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index > 0 }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_live_preserved_prototype_count = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_scan_index = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_live_preserved_prototype_count > 0 }
			NOT = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden }
		}
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_live_preserved_prototype_count < 1 }
			has_idea = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden
		}
		remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden
	}
}

# Inputs: infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile and
# infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus. Meta insertion keeps the category
# profile-specific while the bonus remains centrally tuned.
infantry_spawn_grant_prototype_research_insight = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.research_uses }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = armor } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.mechanized_column } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = cat_mechanized_equipment } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.motor_rifle_formation } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = motorized_equipment } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.assault_gun_group } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = artillery } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_car_screen } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = cat_armored_cars } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.air_mobile_detachment } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = support_tech } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.amphibious_reserve } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = cat_mechanized_equipment } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.heavy_support_formation } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = artillery } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.single_arm_curiosity } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_tech_bonus = { name = infantry_spawn_prototype_research_insight bonus = [BONUS] uses = [USES] category = land_doctrine } }
			BONUS = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus|2]"
			USES = "[?infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_uses|.0]"
		}
