}

# Input: infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid. This is a non-mutating exact
# identity proof for the trial result. It accepts only the active unit-ledger
# cohort and matching live divisions of the recorded generation and template.
infantry_spawn_prove_prototype_trial_formation = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_valid = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_failed = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_prototype_live_unit_uids
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.fully_random compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.maintenance_trial }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_template_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_template_uid }
		infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists = 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_failed = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index > 0 }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_generation_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_template_uid compare = equals }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index } }
					}
					add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_failed = 1 } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_unit_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = 1 }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_generation_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_template_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.active }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
					NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_prototype_live_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid } }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { infantry_spawn_prototype_live_unit_uids = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_failed = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids^num < 1 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_prototype_live_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_failed = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_failed = 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_valid = 1 }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_start_prototype_maintenance_trial = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unmanaged_prototype_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_started = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			infantry_spawn_can_execute_selected_prototype_preservation = yes
		}
		infantry_spawn_pay_prototype_preservation_cost = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.prototype_trial_base_days }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					num_of_military_factories > constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.industrial_military_factory_floor
					num_of_civilian_factories > constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.industrial_civilian_factory_floor
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.prototype_trial_industrial_discount_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_congestion_is_severe = yes }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.prototype_trial_congestion_penalty_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { fuel_ratio < constant:infantry_spawn_factor.quarter }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.prototype_trial_low_fuel_penalty_days }
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days min = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.prototype_trial_minimum_days max = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.prototype_trial_maximum_days }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.maintenance_trial }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_running
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_prototype_maintenance_trial_mission
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_started = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_started = 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_complete_prototype_maintenance_trial = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	infantry_spawn_prove_prototype_trial_formation = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_proof_valid > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_lot_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { infantry_spawn_control_is_low = yes }
				NOT = { infantry_spawn_congestion_is_severe = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.preserved }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.field_ready compare = less_than } }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.full_success_training_gain }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.exceptional compare = less_than } }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.full_success_quality_gain }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.finite_prototype compare = less_than } }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.full_success_equipment_gain }
			}
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.full_success_control_gain }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_count = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_prototype_insight_bonus = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.full_research_bonus }
			infantry_spawn_grant_prototype_research_insight = yes
			infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_full_success
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_partial_success
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_failed
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.preserved }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.field_ready compare = less_than } }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.partial_success_training_gain }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.partial_success_quality_loss }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.partial_success_equipment_loss }
			clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index min = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.skeletal max = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.exceptional }
			clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index min = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.skeletal max = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.finite_prototype }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.partial_success_control_gain }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_count = 1 }
			infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_management_partial
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_partial_success
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_full_success
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_failed
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.training }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.maintenance_trial }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.unmanaged }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.revealed }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.failure_quality_loss }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.failure_equipment_loss }
			clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index min = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.skeletal max = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.exceptional }
			clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index min = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.skeletal max = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.finite_prototype }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.failure_control_loss }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_congestion_pressure = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype.failure_congestion_pressure }
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_failed
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_full_success
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_prototype_trial_partial_success
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_prototype_expected_unit_uids
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_prototype_live_unit_uids
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_trial_running
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_prototype_target_lot_uid
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_prototype_mission_days
	infantry_spawn_refresh_preserved_prototype_idea = yes
	infantry_spawn_evolution_two_expand_management = yes
	infantry_spawn_muster_board_refresh_if_open = yes
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_cannibalize_selected_prototype = {
	if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } infantry_spawn_select_most_unsustainable_prototype_lot = yes }
	infantry_spawn_capture_selected_lot_uid = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_target_is_valid > 0 }
			infantry_spawn_can_execute_selected_prototype_cannibalization = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_management_target_lot_uid }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_salvage_share = constant:infantry_spawn_accounting.cannibalization_salvage_share }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_specialist_only = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_include_claimant_loyal = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_is_cleanup = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition = constant:infantry_spawn_teardown_disposition.prototype_cannibalization }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_teardown_allow_absent_units = 0 }
		infantry_spawn_execute_exact_lot_teardown = yes
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes }
	infantry_spawn_evolution_two_expand_management = yes
	infantry_spawn_muster_board_refresh_if_open = yes
}

infantry_spawn_evolution_two_expand_management = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_management_available
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_preservation_available
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_cannibalization_available
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_has_evolution_ii = yes }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_evolution_two_management_active
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_formation_request_available
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_reserve_group compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.fully_random compare = less_than }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilizing compare = less_than }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 0 }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index > 0 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.unmanaged } }
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_management_available
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_preservation_available
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_cannibalization_available
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_prototype_disposition.preserved } }
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_management_available
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prototype_cannibalization_available
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_management_scan_index = 1 }
		}
		infantry_spawn_refresh_preserved_prototype_idea = yes
		infantry_spawn_refresh_request_costs = yes
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_evolution_two_management_active
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_formation_request_available
		if = { limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden } remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Evolution II random formation request costs and committed draw
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_refresh_request_costs = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = num_of_controlled_states }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.state_scale }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = infantry_spawn_request_scale_term }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = infantry_spawn_active_division_count }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.event_division_scale }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = infantry_spawn_request_scale_term }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = infantry_spawn_army_congestion }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.congestion_scale }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = infantry_spawn_request_scale_term }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = infantry_spawn_active_lot_count }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.state_scale }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = infantry_spawn_request_scale_term }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_control_shortfall = constant:infantry_spawn_control.maximum }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_control_shortfall = infantry_spawn_muster_control }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_control_shortfall > 0 } }
		divide_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_control_shortfall = constant:infantry_spawn_control.maximum }
		multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_control_shortfall = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.half }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = infantry_spawn_request_control_shortfall }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = infantry_spawn_request_count_generation }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale_term = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.prior_request_scale }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = infantry_spawn_request_scale_term }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.war_discount } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale < constant:infantry_spawn_factor.half } } set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.half } }

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_army_experience }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_infantry_equipment }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_support_equipment }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_trucks }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_trains }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_fuel }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.base_manpower_liability }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_scale }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.mobility_fuel_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_fuel_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.mobility_fuel_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_mobile_truck_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_mobile_fuel_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_manpower_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.modest }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_territorial_xp_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_territorial_truck_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_territorial_manpower_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.firepower_equipment_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.firepower_equipment_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_specialist_infantry_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_specialist_support_cost

	# Evolution III request philosophies all enter the unrestricted component
	# draw, but pay distinct persistent costs so triggers, decisions, and the
	# Muster Board can expose the same transaction without temporary values.
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.numbers_equipment_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_manpower_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.numbers_manpower_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_numbers_infantry_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_numbers_manpower_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.discipline_experience_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.discipline_support_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_discipline_xp_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_discipline_support_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.firepower_equipment_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.firepower_equipment_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_firepower_infantry_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_firepower_support_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.mobility_fuel_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_fuel_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.mobility_fuel_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_mobility_truck_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_mobility_fuel_cost

	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_xp_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_infantry_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_support_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_truck_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_train_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_field_manpower_cost }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_train_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_fuel_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	multiply_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_manpower_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_request_cost.anything_risk_multiplier }
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_xp_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_infantry_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_support_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_truck_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_train_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_fuel_cost
	round_variable = infantry_spawn_request_anything_manpower_cost

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_thousand = constant:infantry_spawn_accounting.manpower_liability_per_battalion }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_thousand = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.double }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobile_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_mobile_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_territorial_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_territorial_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_specialist_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_specialist_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_numbers_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_numbers_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_discipline_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_discipline_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_firepower_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_firepower_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mobility_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_mobility_fuel_cost }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_anything_fuel_cost_k = infantry_spawn_request_anything_fuel_cost }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_field_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_mobile_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_territorial_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_specialist_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_numbers_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_discipline_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_firepower_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_mobility_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
	divide_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_anything_fuel_cost_k value = infantry_spawn_request_thousand if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
}

infantry_spawn_pay_selected_request_costs = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = army_experience value = infantry_spawn_request_xp_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_infantry_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@support_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_support_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@motorized_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_truck_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@train_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_train_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = fuel_k value = infantry_spawn_request_fuel_cost_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = manpower value = infantry_spawn_request_manpower_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_xp_cost }
		army_experience = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_infantry_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_support_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_truck_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_train_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_fuel_cost }
		add_fuel = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = 0 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment = infantry_spawn_request_manpower_cost }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_request_negative_payment
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_build_request_candidate_pool = {
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_family_candidate_profile_entries
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_family_candidate_weight_entries
	infantry_spawn_prepare_current_state_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mode_selection = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.mobile_reserve } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.one_third }
		infantry_spawn_add_organized_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		infantry_spawn_add_arsenal_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.mobile_reserve }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_organized_weight.mobile_reserve }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.motor_rifle_formation }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_arsenal_weight.motor_rifle_formation }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.mechanized_column }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_arsenal_weight.mechanized_column }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_car_screen }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_arsenal_weight.armored_car_screen }
			infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mode_selection = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.territorial_defense } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		infantry_spawn_add_baseline_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.three_quarters }
		infantry_spawn_add_organized_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.garrison_shell }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_baseline_weight.garrison_shell }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.composite_territorial_division }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_organized_weight.composite_territorial_division }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_mode_selection = constant:infantry_spawn_request_mode.specialist } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.one_third }
		infantry_spawn_add_organized_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		infantry_spawn_add_arsenal_family_candidates = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_tech = gw_artillery }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.gun_line_group }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_organized_weight.gun_line_group }
			infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_tech = tech_engineers has_tech = tech_recon }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.heavy_support_formation }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_arsenal_weight.heavy_support_formation }
			infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_tech = interwar_antitank }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.anti_armor_detachment }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_organized_weight.anti_armor_detachment }
			infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_tech = interwar_antiair }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.air_defense_reserve }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_organized_weight.air_defense_reserve }
			infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.quarter }
		infantry_spawn_add_baseline_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		infantry_spawn_add_organized_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.tenth }
		infantry_spawn_add_arsenal_family_candidates = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.line_infantry_regiment }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_organized_weight.line_infantry_regiment }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_risk_weight = infantry_spawn_request_count_generation }
	multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_risk_weight = constant:infantry_spawn_random_template.request_risk_per_prior_request }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_risk_weight > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.single_arm_curiosity }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_candidate_weight = infantry_spawn_request_risk_weight }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_pool_scale = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
		infantry_spawn_add_current_family_candidate = yes
	}
	infantry_spawn_select_current_family_candidate = yes
}

# Captures exact resources before any ordinary or family request debit. The
# structural snapshot below may be taken before or after payment depending on
# the caller, so refund proof owns a separate immutable pre-payment baseline.

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_army_experience = army_experience }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_political_power = political_power }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_manpower = manpower }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_infantry_equipment = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }

# Runs only after every applicable refund hook. Exact equality closes both the
# free-request and double-refund surfaces; a mismatch quarantines the country
# and invalidates the surrounding rollback transaction.
infantry_spawn_verify_management_request_payment_resources_restored = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_refund_valid = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = army_experience value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_army_experience compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = political_power value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_political_power compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = command_power value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_command_power compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = manpower value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_manpower compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = fuel_k value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_fuel_k compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_infantry_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@support_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_support_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_gorilla_heavy_infantry_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_gorilla_heavy_infantry_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_pan_sappers_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_pan_sappers_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_stone_cohorts_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_stone_cohorts_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_riverborn_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_riverborn_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_forest_giants_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_forest_giants_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_oracle_recon_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_oracle_recon_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_gas_mask_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_decontamination_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_cbrn_instrument_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@clone_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_clone_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_robot_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_kruger_robot_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@teleportation_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_teleportation_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_temporal_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_kruger_temporal_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@motorized_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_motorized_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@train_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_train_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@coal_golem_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_pre_payment_coal_golem_equipment compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_refund_valid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid = 0 }
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

# Snapshots every mutable structural surface touched by a paid request.
# Monotonic UID allocators are never rewound; rollback deletes their engine
# objects and leaves harmless identity gaps, while successful commits prove
	# Family spawn callbacks overwrite these with their manifest contract; the
	# zero defaults keep the shared materialization proof safe for ordinary and
	# training-only requests that never publish provider rows.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_provider_manifest_row_count = constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_provider_manifest_manpower = constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_provider_manifest_equipment_debt = constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_provider_obligation_row_count = constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
# that every allocator advanced by exactly its published row delta.

# Restores prototype stockpile archetypes that the finite-package generator
# may grant after the request debit but before materialization proof.
infantry_spawn_restore_management_request_prototype_stockpiles = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_restore_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_artillery_equipment }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_restore_delta = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_request_restore_delta producer = THIS }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_restore_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_light_tank_chassis }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_restore_delta = num_equipment@light_tank_chassis }

# Removes all rows and engine objects created after the structural snapshot,
# restores the reusable generation tail, prototype stockpiles, and aggregates,
# then proves the structural state. Callers refund only after this proof and
# separately compare every debited resource with the pre-payment snapshot.

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_unit_index = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_unit_count }

		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_rollback_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }

		delete_unit = { id = infantry_spawn_request_rollback_delete_cohort_id disband = no }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_unit_index = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_template_uid = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_template_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_template_uid = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_template_end = global.infantry_spawn_next_template_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_template_end = 1 }

				text = { delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" disband = no } }
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_request_rollback_template_uid|.0]"

			infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > 0 } } set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid = 0 } }
