				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_division_count compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_generation_uid compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_lot_uid compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_template_uid compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_unit_uid compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_obligation_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_obligation_uid compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_delete_cohort_id compare = less_than }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@artillery_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_artillery_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@light_tank_chassis value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_light_tank_chassis compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@mechanized_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_mechanized_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@armored_car_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_armored_car_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@helicopter_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_helicopter_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_chaosx_elephant_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_gorilla_heavy_infantry_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_gorilla_heavy_infantry_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_pan_sappers_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_pan_sappers_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_stone_cohorts_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_stone_cohorts_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_riverborn_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_riverborn_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_forest_giants_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_forest_giants_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_oracle_recon_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_oracle_recon_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_disaster_wardens_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@gas_mask_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_gas_mask_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@decontamination_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_decontamination_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@cbrn_instrument_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_cbrn_instrument_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@clone_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_clone_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_robot_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_kruger_robot_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_kruger_paleogenetic_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_kruger_xenobiological_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_kruger_exotic_arms_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@teleportation_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_teleportation_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@kruger_temporal_equipment_1 value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_kruger_temporal_equipment_1 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@amphibious_mechanized_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_amphibious_mechanized_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@anti_tank_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_anti_tank_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@anti_air_equipment value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_anti_air_equipment compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_current_family_training_authorized value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_family_training_authorized compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_request_current_family_reinforcement_spawned value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_family_reinforcement_spawned compare = equals } }
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid > 0 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_ledger_invariant_failure > 0 } } set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
		else = { clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_processing_deferred > 0 } } set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_processing_deferred }
		else = { clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_processing_deferred }
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes }
}


# Public paid-request dispatch. Evolution II keeps its weighted serious,
# advanced, and strange family profiles. Evolution III alone crosses into the
# unrestricted component registry, where quality and coherence roll separately.
infantry_spawn_dispatch_management_request_lot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_generation_succeeded = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_context_ready > 0 } }
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_state_is_eligible = yes }
			save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state
		}
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_has_evolution_iii = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_mode value = infantry_spawn_request_mode_selection }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_context.country_request }
			set_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_request_command_owner value = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.state }
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_draw_requested_lot = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag_ids
						value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_outcome_tag.draw_complete
					}
				}
				infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialize_drawn_lot = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_materialization_succeeded }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_generation_succeeded = 1 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_profile.arsenal }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			infantry_spawn_build_request_candidate_pool = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_profile > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
				infantry_spawn_create_current_ordinary_lot = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
					infantry_spawn_append_selected_state_row = yes
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_selected_state_count = 1 }
					infantry_spawn_roll_units_for_current_state = yes
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_state_unit_index = 0 }
					while_loop_effect = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_state_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_current_state_unit_target compare = less_than }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
						}
						infantry_spawn_spawn_current_template_unit = yes
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_state_unit_index = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
						infantry_spawn_update_current_generation_totals = yes
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_generation_succeeded = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_prepare_management_request_allocator_expectations = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_generation_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_generation_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_generation_count }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_generation_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_request_generation_allocator_delta }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_lot_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_lot_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_lot_count }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_lot_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_request_lot_allocator_delta }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_template_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_template_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_template_count }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_template_uid = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_template_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_template_uid = infantry_spawn_request_template_allocator_delta }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_unit_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_unit_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_unit_count }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_unit_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_request_unit_allocator_delta }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_delete_cohort_id = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_delete_cohort_id }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_delete_cohort_id = infantry_spawn_request_unit_allocator_delta }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_obligation_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_obligation_allocator_delta = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_obligation_count }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_obligation_uid = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_next_obligation_uid }
	add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_obligation_uid = infantry_spawn_request_obligation_allocator_delta }
}

infantry_spawn_verify_management_request_materialization = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_transaction_committed = 0 }
	infantry_spawn_prepare_management_request_allocator_expectations = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_unit_row = yes
	infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
	infantry_spawn_validate_all_ledgers = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_generation_succeeded > 0 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_lot_count compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_template_count compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_component_count compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_selected_state_generation_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_selected_state_count compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_unit_count compare = greater_than }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id > constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_provider_manifest_row_count = constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_obligation_count compare = equals }
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_provider_manifest_row_count > constant:infantry_spawn_muster_gui_value.zero }
					}
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_obligation_count compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_request_snapshot_division_count compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_generation_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_lot_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_template_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_unit_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_obligation_uid value = infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_obligation_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_request_expected_next_delete_cohort_id compare = equals }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid compare = equals }
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_live_division
			event_target:infantry_spawn_current_live_division = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_transaction_committed = 1 }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_request_random_formation = {
	infantry_spawn_initialize_global_system = yes
	infantry_spawn_initialize_country_system = yes
	infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_transaction_committed = 0 }
	infantry_spawn_snapshot_management_request_payment_resources = yes
	infantry_spawn_snapshot_management_request_transaction = yes
	infantry_spawn_prepare_request_generation_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_context_ready > 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_refresh_request_costs = yes
		infantry_spawn_load_selected_request_costs = yes
		infantry_spawn_pay_selected_request_costs = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_xp_cost = infantry_spawn_request_xp_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_infantry_cost = infantry_spawn_request_infantry_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_support_cost = infantry_spawn_request_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_truck_cost = infantry_spawn_request_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_train_cost = infantry_spawn_request_train_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_fuel_cost = infantry_spawn_request_fuel_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_manpower_cost = infantry_spawn_request_manpower_cost }
			infantry_spawn_dispatch_management_request_lot = yes
			infantry_spawn_verify_management_request_materialization = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_transaction_committed > 0 } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_muster_board_dormant
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_prior_count = infantry_spawn_request_count_generation }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.request_base_cooldown_days }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_extension = infantry_spawn_request_prior_count }
		multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_extension = constant:infantry_spawn_timer.request_cooldown_per_prior_request_days }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_days = infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_extension }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_count_generation = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
			add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_requests = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_last_request_mode = infantry_spawn_request_mode_selection }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_request_cooldown
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_mission
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_control.request_penalty }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_request_succeeded
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_request_failed
		infantry_spawn_record_management_success = yes
	}
	else = {
		infantry_spawn_rollback_management_request_transaction = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded > 0 } }
				infantry_spawn_refund_management_request_costs = yes
			}
			infantry_spawn_verify_management_request_payment_resources_restored = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_request_failed
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_last_request_succeeded
		infantry_spawn_record_management_failure = yes
	}
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
	infantry_spawn_refresh_request_costs = yes
}

infantry_spawn_finish_request_cooldown = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_request_cooldown
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_request_cooldown_days
	infantry_spawn_refresh_request_costs = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Same-tag transaction-safe mission completion replay
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Timeout effects capture immutable lot/state identity while a scenario
# transaction owns the country. The public resume effect resolves every record
# independently after the transaction returns to idle, so simultaneous mission
# expirations cannot overwrite or suppress one another.

infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = {
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_invariant_failure
	infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_defer_or_complete_selected_lot_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_audit = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_completion_pending } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_completion_pending
	}
}

infantry_spawn_defer_or_complete_selected_lot_standardization = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_standardization = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_completion_pending } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_completion_pending
	}
}

infantry_spawn_defer_or_complete_supervised_demobilization = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_complete_supervised_demobilization = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_completion_pending } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_completion_pending
	}
}

infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_audit_completion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_completion_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_decision_running }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_lot_uid
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_lot_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_lot_uid }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			}
			infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_audit = yes
		}
		else = {
			infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_audit_organized_success_package
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_audit_target_lot_uid
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_completion_pending
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_audit_lot_uid
	}
}

infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_standardization_completion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_completion_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_standardization_running }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_lot_uid
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_lot_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_lot_uid }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			}
			infantry_spawn_complete_selected_lot_standardization = yes
		}
		else = {
			infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_common_tables_cycle
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_standardization_target_lot_uid
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_completion_pending
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_standardization_lot_uid
	}
}

infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_demobilization_completion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_completion_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_demobilization_running }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_lot_uid
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_lot_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_lot_uid }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			}
			infantry_spawn_complete_supervised_demobilization = yes
		}
		else = {
			infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_specialist_preservation_running } } clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_selected_lot_demobilization_pending }
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_demobilization_target_lot_uid
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_completion_pending
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_demobilization_lot_uid
	}
}

			infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_rail_corridor_target_state
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_rail_corridor_completion_pending
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_rail_corridor_target_state
	}
}

# Public mission-only replay entry point. The parent transaction release and the
# bounded Event 19 pulse may both call it; every child effect is idempotent.
infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_management_completions = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_audit_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_standardization_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_demobilization_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_training_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_muster_districts_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_integration_staff_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_specialist_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_prototype_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_rail_corridor_completion = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_request_cooldown_completion = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ledger-backed formation incidents
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_select_latest_incident_lot = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilized compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index > 0 }
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_lot_incident_count_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index
					value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.maximum_per_lot
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = infantry_spawn_incident_scan_index }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_incident_clamp_lot_and_country = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index_is_valid = yes }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.improvised
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_training_state.field_ready
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.skeletal
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_material_quality.exceptional
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.catastrophic
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_coherence.coherent
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.skeletal
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_state.finite_prototype
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_supply_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.light
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_supply_burden.locally_impossible
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_susceptibility_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = 0
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.claimant_susceptibility_maximum
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = infantry_spawn_lot_demobilization_resistance_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			min = 0
			max = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.demobilization_resistance_maximum
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index < 0 } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = 0 }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = infantry_spawn_muster_control
		min = constant:infantry_spawn_control.minimum
		max = constant:infantry_spawn_control.maximum
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = infantry_spawn_army_congestion
		min = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.minimum
		max = constant:infantry_spawn_congestion.maximum
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt < 0 } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = 0 }
	}
}

# Input: infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount. Settles the exact matching lot's
# obligation rows in order, so country debt, lot debt, paid amount, and status
# remain aligned rather than applying an accounting-only discount.
infantry_spawn_incident_settle_lot_debt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_remaining = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_target_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index = 0 }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_remaining > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index value = infantry_spawn_incident_target_lot_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index > 0 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.outstanding }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.servicing }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_row_debt_before = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_payment = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_remaining value = infantry_spawn_incident_row_debt_before compare = less_than } }
					multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_payment = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_remaining }
					divide_temp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_payment value = infantry_spawn_incident_row_debt_before if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_factor.standard }
					round_temp_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_payment
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_payment_index = infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_payment_lot_index = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_payment_amount = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_payment }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_payment_is_material = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_payment_applied = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_payment > 0 } }
					infantry_spawn_apply_exact_obligation_payment = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_payment_applied > 0 } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_relief_applied = infantry_spawn_incident_row_debt_before }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_relief_applied = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_remaining = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_relief_applied }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_remaining = 0 } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_obligation_index = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Input: infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount. Adds a real infantry-equipment
# obligation tied to the selected lot instead of changing the aggregate alone.
infantry_spawn_incident_add_lot_debt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_resource_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.infantry_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_issued = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_paid = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_outstanding = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_status = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.outstanding }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_debt_value_override = infantry_spawn_incident_debt_amount }
		infantry_spawn_append_current_obligation_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_obligation_debt_value_override = 0 }
	}
}

# Inputs: infantry_spawn_incident_resource_cost and the relevant fixed helper.
infantry_spawn_incident_pay_infantry_equipment = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = infantry_spawn_incident_resource_cost }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost }
}

infantry_spawn_incident_pay_support_equipment = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = infantry_spawn_incident_resource_cost }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost }
}

infantry_spawn_incident_pay_motorized_equipment = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = infantry_spawn_incident_resource_cost }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost }
}

infantry_spawn_incident_pay_army_experience = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = 0 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost = infantry_spawn_incident_resource_cost }
	add_army_experience = infantry_spawn_incident_negative_cost
}

infantry_spawn_finish_lot_incident = {
	infantry_spawn_incident_clamp_lot_and_country = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_incident_pending
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.cooldown_days }
	set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_incident_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_incident_cooldown_days }
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_cooldown_days
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_last_incident_date = global.date }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_total_lot_incidents } }
		set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_lot_incidents = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_lot_incidents = 1 }
	}
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid
	infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
}

infantry_spawn_dispatch_lot_incident = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_can_roll_lot_incident = yes }
		infantry_spawn_select_latest_incident_lot = yes
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index_is_valid = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_roll_chance = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.pulse_chance_baseline }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index > constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.garrison_shell } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_roll_chance = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.pulse_chance_organized }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index > constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.composite_territorial_division } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_roll_chance = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.pulse_chance_arsenal }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_roll_succeeded = 0 }
			random = {
				chance = infantry_spawn_incident_roll_chance
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_roll_succeeded = 1 }
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_incident_pending
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_incident_count_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = 1 }
				var:infantry_spawn_lot_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = {
					save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_incident_state
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.garrison_shell compare = less_than_or_equals } }
					random_list = {
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.300 } }
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.301 } }
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.302 } }
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.303 } }
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.304 } }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.composite_territorial_division compare = less_than_or_equals } }
					random_list = {
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.305 } }
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.306 } }
						@infantry_spawn_incident_equal_weight = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.307 } }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.air_mobile_detachment } }
					country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.309 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.armored_car_screen } }
					country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.312 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index > 2 } }
					country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.310 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
							value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.mobility_mismatch_incident
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.311 }
				}
				else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.308 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_roll_succeeded < 1 } }
				clear_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_incident_barracks_local_authority = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index_is_valid = yes }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.local_authority }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.territorial }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.control_small }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_susceptibility_entries^infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.claimant_susceptibility_small }
	}

	}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Same-tag transaction-safe incident choice replay
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The incident choice enum is intentionally contiguous and centralized here so
# every report option stores one exact immutable selection:
#  1 barracks_local_authority       2 barracks_regular_army
#  3 barracks_provisional_committee 4 ammunition_divert_production
#  5 ammunition_cannibalize         6 ammunition_delay
#  7 motor_pool_territorial         8 motor_pool_rebuild
#  9 motor_pool_accept_burden      10 village_territorial_service
# 11 village_enforce_transfer      12 village_supply_bargain
# 13 officers_regularize           14 officers_disperse
# 15 officers_preserve_cadre       16 staff_merge
# 17 staff_favor_regular           18 staff_reserve_command
# 19 depot_absorb                  20 depot_preserve
# 21 depot_strip                   22 colors_recognize
# 23 colors_refuse                 24 colors_territorial
# 25 tanks_engineers               26 tanks_one_offensive
# 27 tanks_dismantle               28 rotorcraft_maintain
# 29 rotorcraft_ceremonial         30 rotorcraft_field
# 31 radios_standardize            32 radios_accept_penalty
# 33 radios_split_command          34 cavalry_keep_horses
# 35 cavalry_force_trucks          36 cavalry_mixed_doctrine
# 37 armored_cars_recon            38 armored_cars_retrain
# 39 armored_cars_preserve

infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_incident_choice_record = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_pending
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid
}

infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_incident_choice = {
	infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_incident_pending
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid
	infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_incident_choice_record = yes
}

infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_check_affordability = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.ammunition_divert_production }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.infantry_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.motor_pool_rebuild }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@motorized_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.motorized_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.village_supply_bargain }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@support_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.support_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.tanks_engineers }
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@support_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.support_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = army_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.army_experience_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.rotorcraft_maintain }
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@support_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.support_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@motorized_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.motorized_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.radios_standardize }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@support_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.support_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.cavalry_force_trucks }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_equipment@motorized_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.motorized_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.armored_cars_retrain }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = army_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_incident.army_experience_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_execute_deferred_incident_choice = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.barracks_local_authority } }
		infantry_spawn_incident_barracks_local_authority = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.barracks_regular_army } }
		infantry_spawn_incident_barracks_regular_army = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.barracks_provisional_committee } }
		infantry_spawn_incident_barracks_provisional_committee = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.ammunition_divert_production } }
		infantry_spawn_incident_ammunition_divert_production = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.ammunition_cannibalize } }
		infantry_spawn_incident_ammunition_cannibalize = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.ammunition_delay } }
