infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_incident_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_incident_pending } }
			infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_incident_choice = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_record_is_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice }
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid }
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice > constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.none } }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice > constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice.armored_cars_preserve }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid compare = equals } }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_record_is_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_record_is_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid }
				infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.demobilized compare = less_than }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > 0 }
					}
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_incident_lot_index = infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_check_affordability = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_affordable = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set } }
						infantry_spawn_execute_deferred_incident_choice = yes
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
								NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_incident_pending }
							}
							infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_incident_choice_record = yes
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set } }
							infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_incident_choice = yes
						}
						else = { infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_incident_choice = yes }
					}
				}
				else = { infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_incident_choice = yes }
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_incident_choice = yes }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_defer_or_resolve_incident_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_incident_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_pending }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice = infantry_spawn_incident_choice_request }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_incident_lot_uid }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_incident_choice_pending
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_incident_choice = yes
	}
}

# Prefire choice 1 is the Evolution III opening draw; choice 2 is decline.
infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_record = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_pending
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice
}

infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = {
	infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prefire_opening_pending
	infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_record = yes
}

infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prefire_opening_pending } }
			infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_choice.draw } }
				infantry_spawn_execute_prefire_evolution_iii_initial_draw = yes
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_choice.decline } }
				infantry_spawn_decline_prefire_evolution_iii_initial_draw = yes
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prefire_opening_pending }
				}
				infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_record = yes
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = yes }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_defer_or_resolve_prefire_opening_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_prefire_opening_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_pending }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = infantry_spawn_prefire_opening_choice_request }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_prefire_opening_choice_pending
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = yes
	}
}

# Claimant choice 1 accepts; choice 2 refuses. Replay re-resolves the immutable
# claimant UID, proves the same demand enum, and restores the stable-view
# selection after executing the original response effect.
infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_record = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_pending
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand
}

infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = {
	infantry_spawn_quarantine_pending_claimant_demand = yes
	infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_record = yes
}

infantry_spawn_restore_selected_claimant_after_deferred_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_had_selected_index = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_previous_selected_index }
	}
	else = { clear_variable = infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
}

infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_pending } }
			infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_record_is_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice }
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid }
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand }
						NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_owner_uid }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice > constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice.none } }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice > constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice.refuse }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_owner_uid value = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid compare = equals } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand > constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_demand.none } }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand > constant:infantry_spawn_generalissimo_demand.parallel_command }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_record_is_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_record_is_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid }
				infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_owner_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand compare = equals }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_had_selected_index = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_had_selected_index = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_previous_selected_index = infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
					}
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.unset }
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice.accept }
							infantry_spawn_can_accept_selected_claimant_demand = yes
						}
						infantry_spawn_accept_selected_claimant_demand = yes
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice.refuse }
							infantry_spawn_can_refuse_selected_claimant_demand = yes
						}
						infantry_spawn_refuse_selected_claimant_demand = yes
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
					}
					infantry_spawn_restore_selected_claimant_after_deferred_choice = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_pending }
						}
						infantry_spawn_clear_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_record = yes
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice.accept }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice_executed = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_replay_marker.set }
							NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_demand.another_formation } }
						}
						infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = constant:infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_choice.refuse } }
						infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes
					}
				}
				else = { infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes }
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_quarantine_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_defer_or_resolve_claimant_demand_choice = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_pending }
		infantry_spawn_select_pending_claimant_demand_owner = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_pending }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_choice_request }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_demand.none }
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_owns_pending_demand = yes }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_mark_deferred_replay_invariant_failure = yes }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_deferred_claimant_demand_choice_pending
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes
	}
}

# Public aggregate replay entry point. The parent transaction release calls it
# after stable-view restoration; the bounded Event 19 pulse supplies a backstop.
infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_event19_actions = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes }
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_prefire_opening_choice = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_claimant_demand_choice = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_incident_choice = yes
		infantry_spawn_resume_deferred_management_completions = yes
	}
}

# Public country pulse / AI-parity hook. It does no global iteration and is
# safe for the Event 19 country pulse to call repeatedly.
infantry_spawn_run_country_management_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_country_is_active = yes
			infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
		}
		infantry_spawn_initialize_management_telemetry = yes
		infantry_spawn_close_resolved_generations = yes
		if = { limit = { infantry_spawn_has_evolution_i = yes } infantry_spawn_evolution_one_reevaluate_management = yes }
		if = { limit = { infantry_spawn_has_evolution_ii = yes } infantry_spawn_evolution_two_expand_management = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { infantry_spawn_selected_lot_is_unresolved = yes } }
			infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { infantry_spawn_congestion_is_severe = yes }
				infantry_spawn_select_weakest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_war = yes }
				infantry_spawn_select_strongest_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_select_first_unresolved_ordinary_lot = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			infantry_spawn_select_first_affordable_exact_obligation_lot = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_exact_affordable_lot_found > 0 } }
				infantry_spawn_settle_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes
			}
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_refresh_selected_lot_exact_obligations = yes }
		infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
		infantry_spawn_dispatch_lot_incident = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ordinary-country annex cleanup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# on_annex supplies event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country. Exact global
# unit UIDs and delete-cohort IDs are used to remove only formations still
# owned by the annexed Event 19 ledger, including formations the engine has
# already handed to the annexer. A failed absence proof leaves the removed
# country quarantined with its ledger intact instead of silently releasing a
# stale package into a later tag restoration.

infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_state_markers = {
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_ordinary_marker_owner
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = infantry_spawn_claimant_headquarters_state_entries
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_autonomous_district
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_claimant_autonomous_district_owner }
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_claimant_autonomous_district_owner
						value = event_target:infantry_spawn_ordinary_marker_owner
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_autonomous_district
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_claimant_autonomous_district_owner
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_state_entries
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_owner }
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_owner
						value = event_target:infantry_spawn_ordinary_marker_owner
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_owner
		}
	}
	clear_array = infantry_spawn_reserved_rail_corridor_state_entries
}

infantry_spawn_remove_annexed_country_claimants = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_index
				value = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_index
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = { remove_unit_leader = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
			CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_uid|.0]"
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = infantry_spawn_annexing_country
				event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = { exists = yes }
			}
			event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { remove_unit_leader = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
					CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_uid|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_lock_annexed_country_templates = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index
				value = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid
						value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "National Muster Standard" force_allow_recruiting = no }
				set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "National Muster Standard" is_locked = yes }
			}
			else = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" force_allow_recruiting = no }
						set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" is_locked = yes }
					}
					TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_prepare_annex_cleanup_unit_set = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_set_frozen } }
		clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids
		clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts
		clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_template_uids
		if = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
				infantry_spawn_compaction_auxiliary_evidence_is_aligned = yes
				infantry_spawn_scenario_transaction_is_idle = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
						value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = {
									var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
									value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.untracked
									compare = greater_than
								}
								check_variable = {
									var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
									value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transferred_out
									compare = less_than
								}
							}
							check_variable = {
								var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
								value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.unaccounted
								compare = equals
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
							NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index } }
							NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts value = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index } }
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids
							value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts
							value = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = {
									var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
									value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transferred_out
									compare = less_than
								}
							}
							set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_scope_identity_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
							set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index }
							set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_delete_cohort = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index }
							var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = {
								if = {
									limit = {
										has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
										has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
										has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id
										check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
										check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_unit_uid compare = equals }
										check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_delete_cohort compare = equals }
									}
									set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_scope_identity_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
								}
							}
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_scope_identity_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
								set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
							}
						}
					}
					else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			every_country_division = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
							NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid } }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_canonical_template_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index
						value = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_template_uids value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid } }
					}
					add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_template_uids value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid compare = equals }
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_canonical_template_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					}
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_canonical_template_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid }
					has_template = "National Muster Standard"
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_set_frozen
		}
		else = {
			clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids
			clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts
			clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_template_uids
		}
	}
	else = {
		# A retry must use the immutable set that existed before any destructive
		# pass. Re-running preflight after a partial delete would discard proof.
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_delete_annex_cleanup_unit_set = {
	# Stored division scopes remain exact when ownership changes. Destroying
	# their verified UID/cohort identities first covers third-country ownership
	# anomalies without iterating the world.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
				value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids
					value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_delete_cohort = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_unit_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_delete_cohort compare = equals }
					}
					destroy_unit = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
				value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_delete_cohort_id = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
		}
		delete_unit = { id = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_delete_cohort_id disband = no }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = infantry_spawn_annexing_country
				event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = { exists = yes }
			}
			event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = {
				delete_unit = { id = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_delete_cohort_id disband = no }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}

	# The division-scope pass is an exact UID cleanup, not a ratio or blanket
	# transfer. It catches units whose ownership changed before on_annex ran.
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
		}
		destroy_unit = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_annexing_country
			event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = { exists = yes }
		}
		event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = {
			every_country_division = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
				}
				destroy_unit = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_prove_annex_cleanup_unit_set_absent = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_units = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
				value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids
					value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_delete_cohort = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_unit_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_expected_delete_cohort compare = equals }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_annexing_country
			event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = { exists = yes }
		}
		event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = {
			every_country_division = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_delete_annexed_country_templates = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index
				value = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid
					value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "National Muster Standard" disband = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" disband = no } }
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid|.0]"
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_prove_annexed_country_templates_absent = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_templates = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index
				value = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid
					value = infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid
					compare = equals
				}
				has_template = "National Muster Standard"
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_templates = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_uid }
			infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_templates = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_template_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_finalize_annexed_ordinary_country_cleanup = {
	infantry_spawn_unregister_participant_evolution_memberships = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_evolution_participant_unregister_succeeded > 0 } }
		infantry_spawn_commit_annexed_ordinary_country_cleanup = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
	}
}

infantry_spawn_commit_annexed_ordinary_country_cleanup = {
	clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_unit_uids
	clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_delete_cohorts
	clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_target_template_uids
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_set_frozen
	infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_state_markers = yes
	infantry_spawn_remove_annexed_country_claimants = yes
	infantry_spawn_remove_ordinary_country_runtime_ideas_and_missions = yes
	infantry_spawn_achievement_abort_country_attempts = yes
	infantry_spawn_clear_exact_profile_totals = yes
	infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_private_ledgers = yes
	infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_auxiliary_arrays = yes
	infantry_spawn_scenario_clear_actor_bypass = yes
	infantry_spawn_scenario_clear_same_tag_snapshot = yes
	infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_runtime_flags = yes
	infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_runtime_variables = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_participant
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_defeat_cleanup_in_progress
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_country_cleanup_complete
}

infantry_spawn_cleanup_annexed_ordinary_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_participant
			NOT = { is_infantry_spawn_derivative_country = yes }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_country_cleanup_complete }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_defeat_cleanup_in_progress
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
		infantry_spawn_abort_prototype_maintenance_trial = yes
		infantry_spawn_prepare_annex_cleanup_unit_set = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
			infantry_spawn_lock_annexed_country_templates = yes
			infantry_spawn_delete_annex_cleanup_unit_set = yes
			infantry_spawn_prove_annex_cleanup_unit_set_absent = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_units = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
				infantry_spawn_delete_annexed_country_templates = yes
				infantry_spawn_prove_annexed_country_templates_absent = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_templates = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
					infantry_spawn_finalize_annexed_ordinary_country_cleanup = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_country_cleanup_complete } }
			infantry_spawn_enqueue_annex_cleanup_retry = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the annexer. One delayed chain drains one exact country
# scope at a time; a persistent array, rather than an event target, survives
# the delay and preserves an annexed country's inaccessible runtime evidence.
infantry_spawn_schedule_annex_cleanup_retry = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries^num > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_scheduled }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_scheduled
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_delay = constant:infantry_spawn_event.annex_cleanup_retry_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.916 days = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_delay }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the annexed participant. The caller supplies the current
# annexer as infantry_spawn_annexing_country in the same effect chain.
infantry_spawn_enqueue_annex_cleanup_retry = {
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_annexing_country }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_annexing_country = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries
							value = event_target:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_array = {
					array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries
					value = event_target:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country
				}
			}
			infantry_spawn_schedule_annex_cleanup_retry = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on one annexed Event 19 country. Derivative identity remains
# live on a failed provider cleanup, allowing the same persistent queue to
# dispatch either the derivative proof-gated finalizer or the ordinary one.
infantry_spawn_cleanup_annexed_event19_country = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_infantry_spawn_derivative_country = yes }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_final_cleanup = yes
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_cleanup_annexed_ordinary_country = yes }
}

# COUNTRY scope on the current annexer. The queue head is selected before the
# array is changed, then removed only after the exact country reports the real
# finalizer flag. Absence, loss of states, or an invariant flag is not proof.
infantry_spawn_process_annex_cleanup_retry_queue = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_scheduled
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries^num > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
		save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_annexing_country
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_queue_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		var:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_queue_index = { save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country = { infantry_spawn_cleanup_annexed_event19_country = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_country = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_country_cleanup_complete } }
				remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries index = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_queue_index }
			}
		}
	}
	infantry_spawn_schedule_annex_cleanup_retry = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope on a new annexer. Pending entries owned by the country being
# annexed migrate before that country's own cleanup can clear runtime arrays.
infantry_spawn_migrate_annex_cleanup_retry_queue = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_previous_annex_cleanup_owner
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_next_annex_cleanup_owner
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migration_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_previous_annex_cleanup_owner = {
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migration_index
						value = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries^num
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				var:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries^infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migration_index = {
					save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migrating_country
				}
				event_target:infantry_spawn_next_annex_cleanup_owner = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries
									value = event_target:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migrating_country
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries
							value = event_target:infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migrating_country
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_migration_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			clear_array = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_country_entries
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_retry_scheduled
		}
		event_target:infantry_spawn_next_annex_cleanup_owner = { infantry_spawn_schedule_annex_cleanup_retry = yes }
	}
}
